## 配置 {#configuration}

`host-verified`

组件: `ArmDTPlatform`, `ArmCpu`, `SoftwareAES`, `XnuPrelink`.

### 硬件与依赖

- Format-2 resolver of the shared UniXNU checkout \(schema\_version = 2\).
- UniXNU-sm7675-common SoC layer and the ipados-27.0.1-24A446 OS profile \(iPad17,3, XNU 12377.121.6, arm64e\).
- 24A446 kernelcache kernelcache.decompressed \(sha256 38e6d9fa...\) as the prelink reference.
- Catalog components with the board's fork pins in feeds.toml.

### 配置

- schema\_version2, sm7675 SoC, ipados-27.0.1-24A446 arm64e, uke kit and uke\_ipados27\_defconfig; GPU enabled.
- Four-layer XNU patch series; current Adreno740/Metal/GOP pins are reachable on the user forks. Workspace-only paths belong in ignored local.toml.

### 操作步骤

- ./unixnu defconfig uke\_ipados27\_defconfig -D ../UniXNU-uke-build
- ./unixnu fetch -D ../UniXNU-uke-build &amp;&amp; ./unixnu status --json -D ../UniXNU-uke-build
- ./unixnu build kernel -D ../UniXNU-uke-build &amp;&amp; ./unixnu build payload -D ../UniXNU-uke-build
- ./unixnu build userdata -D ../UniXNU-uke-build --object OS.vroot=&lt;policy&gt;/manifest.bin

### 开发经验

- 2026-11-15: Published source feeds now pin the Adreno740, AdrenoMetal, ArmCpu, XnuPrelink and RamBlock revisions used by the latest archived Uke build.
- 2026-11-09 identity/touch retry starts from local main9198a6f4 and core558a7474 on fix/uke27-identity-touch-retry-20261109. Only J820 product identity and upstream HID pin are retried; board scale2,999B args,5 existing CA fixups,GPU/Wi-Fi/addon/trust/Data inputs remain. Prior experiments preserved separately; no flash.
- The format-1 patch path never applied the shared patches from a cold tree \(the board module shadowed them\); the format-2 series replays the set statelessly.
- Catalog archive recipes equal the board's legacy builder commands; the component aliases keep the old script names working.
- P2 \(gpucomp26\): the compositor's two-plane 'b3a8' frame is scanned by the DPU natively. Plane 0's 4 B/texel colour words \(rows 4\*width apart\) are XRGB2101010, whose SSPP SRC\_FORMAT 0x000276ff is the firmware's own ARGB8888 word 0x000237ff \(unpack 0x03020001\) with alpha bit 8 clear and DX bit 14 set, per the stock dpu\_hw\_sspp\_setup\_format/dpu\_formats.c in the reference kernel; plane 1 is the alpha byte, whose row is the record's stride field. The 34,177,024-byte frame maps only its 27,340,800-byte plane-0 prefix into one slot; the CPU decode remains the fallback when the display-bank window is unavailable.
- gpucomp26 log \(recovery, userdata window, t=16..917 s\): UKE DPU: direct ready fmt=000237ff/000237ff unpack=03020001 tenbit=000276ff; four b3a8 frames mapped by their plane-0 prefix \(direct slot ... bytes=27340800\); UKE DPU: tenbit sample p0=18060180 p0c=37edfb7e alpha=ff/ff -- the alpha plane is 0xFF, so the frame is opaque and the XRGB2101010 word order is the right one. One stall snapshot at t=78.4 s and none after; GPU commit-to-complete still averages 50.9 ms over 8884 commits in 893 s \(about 9.9/s\).
- gpucomp27 ports the upstream display's refresh tick \(GopFramebuffer 33e941a\): startVsync/vsyncTick/armVsync, getRefreshAttributes at slot 301 and setDisplayRefreshProperties in registerService, with the builder binding \_native\_legacy\_vsync\_notify \(0xfffffff005796720\) and \_native\_legacy\_set\_refresh\_properties \(0xfffffff00579ebb8\) after checking the original bytes; board.toml sets sheng-vsync=120 \(armcpu-diagnostics dropped to stay at 987 of 1000 command-line bytes\). The direct path now prints UKE DPU: flip n=... tb=... skip=... fb=... latch=...us max=...us vsync=... every 120 frames and the first eight UKE DPU: skip lines, so the next log separates compositor starvation from display-path loss.

### 完成条件

- Status resolves arch, soc, os and device layers with the intended pins; the output path is out/uke/ipados-27.0.1-24A446/uke\_ipados27/; kernel.macho is MH\_FILESET/arm64e and prelinks against the 24A446 kernelcache.

### 当前范围

- The reported interactive GPU27 symptoms are user-accepted on CRC90B6BC701210D9DB; no broad multi-client stress or cross-cold-boot cache qualification inferred.

### 下一步

- Candidate 1BF4587AE6964D6F is built and packaged but not flashed. It sets this chip's .idle\_ps to false in tools/prepare-ath11k.py, so ath11k\_mac\_op\_start no longer sends WMI\_PDEV\_PARAM\_IDLE\_PS\_CONFIG \(44\) and the firmware is not authorised into the low power recipe this port cannot service. The next device round must read, in order: the 'ath11k debug mask 00000076' line; that 'wmi cmd pdev set param 44' is gone; whether 'htc ep 2 credits got' and 'msi vector 2' resume after 'wmi cmd vdev create'; and if they do, the vdev set param 48/91/34 lines and 'cmd start scan'. If the firmware still asserts in whal\_wmac\_transition\_to\_fw\_control, IDLE\_PS was not the only entry into the recipe and the next candidate should look at vdev\_start\_delay and any other PM authorisation the host sends.

## 固件与启动 {#firmware}

`device-verified`

组件: `DarwinBoot`.

### 硬件与依赖

- Mu-Silicium UEFI checkout \(uefi-uke\) with the ukePkg platform.
- Payload FAT volume UKEESP with kernel.macho, its ADT and the trust cache.

### 配置

- \[firmware\]: config uke, dsc Platforms/Xiaomi/ukePkg/uke.dsc, FD 3 MiB at FD\_BASE 0xa7100000, ENABLE\_SECUREBOOT=1, MEMORY\_PROTECTION=TRUE.

### 操作步骤

- ./unixnu build firmware --skip-preflight --uefi ../uefi-uke -D ../UniXNU-uke-format2
- fastboot boot boot.img \(or flash vendor\_boot+boot on the inactive slot\)

### 开发经验

- The boot manager displays the DT/initrd status through gUkeMemStatusGuid and the previous XNU panic line through the DXE memory driver.
- gUkeMemStatusGuid must be declared under \[Guids\] in both the DEC and the library INF.
- 2026-11-02 source review: live ABL initrd 0xB2D51000..0xB7FFF41C overlaps the former DXE Heap 0xA8000000..0xC3A00000; sheng 66421cb reserves 0xA8000000..0xB8000000. Candidate reserves the handoff before SEC/DXE and starts the heap at 0xBAB00000; built; four partitions flashed and rebooted at 2026-11-02 01:38, user photo confirms kernel fold C43F1F3EC8168A4C and six tail samples, WINDOW delta=0 and overlap=0; later MMU/kernel execution unresolved.

### 完成条件

- The FD builds, the menu boots the payload entry, and the device reaches the XNU entry point.

### 当前范围

- Paired compressed-payload boot/vendor\_boot reached the user-accepted 24A446 GPU system. Other devices, suspend/resume and long repeated-boot stress are not qualified by this report.

### 下一步

- Use the paired UEFI decompression-capable boot and compressed vendor\_boot; preserve raw FAT fallback and runtime overlap checks.

## CPU 与中断 {#cpu}

`device-verified`

组件: `ArmCpu`, `ArmDTPlatform`, `ArmVirtPlatform`.

### 硬件与依赖

- Eight Kryo cores at affinity 0x000 through 0x700 \(SM7675 cliffs\).
- PSCI over SMC for secondary bring-up.

### 配置

- dts/uke.dts: eight CPUs at 0x000..0x700 with E/P clusters, timebase 19200000 and PSCI SMC.
- Active kernel arguments sheng-smp=1 armcpu-spi-ipi=1 sheng-diag=1; built-in armcpu-edge=1 and armcpu-self-ipi=1.

### 操作步骤

- Build kernel and payload with uke\_ipados27\_defconfig, then verify the packaged eight-core ADT and kernel symbols.

### 开发经验

- 2026-11-15: The user reports no further CPU freezes in current use. Earlier TRACE readback UUID 77C55B96 independently shows all eight CPUs continuing IRQ/timer/IPI progress from 184 to 2408 seconds.
- Single-CPU runs are stable past t=877s; SMP died around t=117s in early runs \(SMP/IPI or UFS concurrency\), mitigated by the SPI-IPI path and watchdog policy.

### 完成条件

- All eight cores enter the scheduler and the system stays up under load.

### 当前范围

- The historical 13fbe7af and 4b898e0b freezes remain reproducible evidence for those images. The exact cause of the previous CPU stoppage and prolonged SMP stress coverage remain open.

### 下一步

- Keep TRACE timing diagnostics. Extend long-run idle/wake and Stage Manager coverage, and correlate any recurrence with same-boot per-core IRQ, timer and IPI progress.

## 内存与 DMA {#memory}

`device-verified`

组件: `RamBlock`.

### 硬件与依赖

- High DRAM window at 0x8C0000000 sized 0x240000000 \(9 GiB\) with the owned window at 0x130000000.
- GMU pool 0xC8000000/32 MiB reserved in the UEFI map and adopted over IODeviceMemory::withRange.

### 配置

- \[payload.adt\]: 0x180000000 high bootstrap window, 16 loader outside-memory entries, chip\_id 207, cpu\_regs 0..0x700, boot-manifest-hash pinned.
- dts/uke.dts memory node: one contiguous range, as the ADT hand-off requires.

### 操作步骤

- Build the payload and read the UKE MEM counters from the menu and the kernel log.

### 开发经验

- owned\_memory\_size, not dram\_size, sizes the XNU VM; the 320 MiB ceiling was lifted by the static high-bank MemoryMapLib entries \(UKE MEM \~1.11M 4K pages, free \~645 MiB\).
- A sheng-style external abort can appear at the 6 GiB top; the full 6 GiB board map is configured in board.toml.
- The outside-window VM aperture uses reserved addresses below the kernel static region. The default high-window profile adopts the lower free DRAM listed by DarwinBoot and selects gpu-pool=0.

### 完成条件

- The kernel owns the 4.75 GiB window and jetsam no longer fires at the old ceiling.

### 当前范围

- Host-only verification of the migrated ADT; the on-device counters come from the pre-migration images.

### 下一步

- After fresh authorization deploy paired6D31A137E8F25F63,regress first/second boot and&gt;8minStageManager load,GPUretire/job/kick,pool tracked/closed/returned,CPUedges,UFS andbattery/Wi-Fi/display/backlight/touch. Preserve34DE boot reference andD4EFbattery rollback.

## 存储与根卷 {#storage}

`device-verified`

组件: `ShengUfs`, `RamBlock`, `VirtioMmioBlock`, `VirtioMmioConsole`, `VerifiedRoot`.

### 硬件与依赖

- UFS host at 0x1d84000 \(GCC windows 0x177000/0x19e000\).
- 24A446 OS disk image: raw APFS, 9,613,344,768 B, image sha256 8a12cdad... \(equals profile \[root\]\), volume UUID 2F8E6EED-...; the VerifiedRoot OS policy verified all 2,347,008 blocks.
- userdata SHNGROOT v2 image \(9,689,890,816 B\): OS window at 0x100000 \(block 512, read-only\) and the OS.vroot data object at 0x23D100000 \(75,104,800 B, block 4096\).

### 配置

- Source storage: ShengUfs + VerifiedRoot + RamBlock + VirtioMmioBlock; the 544-byte policy header travels as kernel.macho.vroot and the hash table as the OS.vroot userdata object.
- Kernel arguments native-source-storage=1 native-ufs-root=1; there is no unverified transport on this line.

### 操作步骤

- ./unixnu build kernel -D ../UniXNU-uke-build
- ./unixnu build userdata -D ../UniXNU-uke-build --object OS.vroot=&lt;policy&gt;/manifest.bin

### 开发经验

- The display bank's stage-1 window functions \(native\_ufs\_dpu\_map/table/l2\) belong to ShengUfs; the display builder stubs only their data when the source storage is absent, so the display backend and the storage link into one kernel \(the map stub is compiled only without storage\).
- The root policy hashes every 4 KiB block of the OS image: the host verification ran vr\_host\_block over all 2,347,008 blocks and the image sha256 equals the profile root \(8a12cdad...\); the 592-byte kernel.macho.vroot bundle's 544-byte manifest header is byte-identical to manifest.bin.
- The OS member of the IPSW is an 8.25 GB AEA archive that decrypts to the raw APFS image; on Linux build userdata reuses it through a .dmg.json cache record instead of the macOS aea tool \(rules 7.22\).

### 完成条件

- The kernel mounts the OS volume from the userdata window through the verified policy and bsd\_init reaches launchd.

### 当前范围

- Original OS/SystemOS and persistent Data were used for the accepted GPU27 system. Power-loss recovery/failure injection was not newly tested.

### 下一步

- After fresh authorization, compare Data FUA and flush progress with GPU fences and display latch; verify synced Data after reboot.

## 显示 {#display}

`device-verified`

组件: `GopFramebuffer`, `NativeUfs`.

### 硬件与依赖

- UEFI GOP framebuffer; panel O82 3200x2136 with the splash at 0xe3940000/0x2b00000.
- Overlay-present Legacy driver and the recorded mobile-graphics ABI candidates.
- DPU track: cliffs DTB mdss\_mdp@0xae00000 \(sde-off 0x1000, VIG 0x5000/0x7000, CTL0 0x16000, INTF1/2 0x36000/0x37000, PP0 0x6a000, LM0 0x45000\), apps\_smmu 0x15000000 display streams SID 0x1c00 mask 0x02.

### 配置

- Kernel arguments offline-legacy-\* \(register, gop, phase 4\), offline-mobile-graphics-load=1.
- offline-legacy-base-present=1 and offline-legacy-noconsole=1 gate the own-present path \(the gpu-framepresent v2/v3 verified set\); without base-present presentBase returns at its first line and the screen stays on the boot frame.
- The GPU track arguments \(sheng-gpu-power=9, sheng-gpu-metal=1, offline-mobile-graphics-client=1, sheng-gpu-pool=0, sheng-fb-gpu=0\) restore the compositor's 1200-byte overlay records whose layer 6 frame the own-present path resolves.
- The default-surface fallback is disabled so only resolved overlay surfaces present.
- DPU track: uke-dpu=&lt;1\|2&gt; runs the read-only live DPU snapshot \(default off\); uke-dpu-test=&lt;1..4&gt; runs the bounded controlled-pipe test \(default off\); uke-dpu-scanout&gt;=1 builds the display bank's stage-1 window in NativeUfs \(default off\).

### 操作步骤

- Build the legacy display archive and boot with the offline-legacy arguments.

### 开发经验

- 2026-11-15: The user confirms brightness adjustment works in current use on the dual KTZ8866 backlight.
- 2026-11-09 exact payload panic symbolication corrects prior AGX diagnosis: PCb404ef4 is OSMetaClassD2+34; LRb629628 is NativeLegacyDisplayD0 after base D2. Legacy fixture incorrectly bound instance D2 a288c6c to metaclass destructor through import836b0e8. Correct original instance D2 a288cac chains viaa2819bc/a2999e0/import836b340 to IOServiceD2. Original kernelcache38e6d9fa and machine-code evidence archived. Fix keeps PAC and normal cleanup; triggering init failure and boot result remain unverified.
- 2026-11-06: build-native-legacy-display-27.py only gained an ArmSmmu4K include path for WifiProbe; display objects and behaviour are unchanged.
- 2026-11-03 ca-surface-track: user explicitly confirms normal upward-swipe entry without the previous freeze; CRC0485290E96ACC9B3/final988d162c/vendor150b5c06. Evidence is user report, not only flash or host checks.
- 2026-11-06 WPSS repair adds its mapped-store header to the shared Legacy archive source hashes; display/GPU/touch implementations and their late-probe startup calls remain unchanged.
- The present chain resolved the overlay layer and put real UI on screen \(white lock screen, home indicator, swipe prompt\).
- A blank after a few seconds was the blank/idle transaction falling back to the pure-black default surface; v3 presents only resolved overlay surfaces.
- 2026-10-28 fmt2 boot: the swap path rejects every frame \(UKE: swap accepted=0 reason=2 surface=0\) and the screen stays on the boot console while the system runs fully in userland; identical to the 2026-10-27 session, so the patch adoption is not the cause.
- 2026-10-28 root cause: those frozen-screen runs never set offline-legacy-base-present=1, so presentBase returned before doing anything and never logged; the accepted=0 reason=2 surface=0 line is the self-kick's pre-translation validation \(the request's +2624 surface slots fill only inside the original swap\_submit\), not an own-present resolution failure.
- presentBase now also honors the family's own second rule from swap\_submit: a record ID equal to the default surface's ID names fb+0x1618 directly \(the CPU-renderer 1288-byte records name that surface on layer 0\).
- The Data seed handoff is closed out: the scratch Data mount panic \(ENOENT at kern\_exec.c:8196\) was a missing PackedRAMDisk/TrustCache handoff in the test payload, fixed by wiring data-seed-200/owner-data-200.dbr and the owner trust cache into unixnu.local.toml.
- DPU archaeology: the cliffs DTS block offsets minus sde-off \(0x1000\) equal the DPU 9.x anchors sheng's DisplayTimingProbe uses \(VIG0/1 0x4000/0x6000, CTL0 0x15000, INTF1/2 0x35000/0x36000, PP0 0x69000, LM0 0x44000\); the stock DPU driver in this drop stops at DPU 7.x, so the DTB plus the live firmware snapshot are the register authority.
- DPU surface addressing: the display streams are SID 0x1c00/0x1c02 with qcom,handoff-smrs in the cliffs apps\_smmu node; the pipe address registers are 32-bit while XNU memory starts at 0x8C0000000, so scanout needs the ShengUfs display-bank stage-1 window \(ported as native\_ufs\_dpu\_map in NativeUfs\).
- P2 \(gpucomp26\): the compositor's two-plane 'b3a8' frame is scanned by the DPU natively. Plane 0's 4 B/texel colour words \(rows 4\*width apart\) are XRGB2101010, whose SSPP SRC\_FORMAT 0x000276ff is the firmware's own ARGB8888 word 0x000237ff \(unpack 0x03020001\) with alpha bit 8 clear and DX bit 14 set, per the stock dpu\_hw\_sspp\_setup\_format/dpu\_formats.c in the reference kernel; plane 1 is the alpha byte, whose row is the record's stride field. The 34,177,024-byte frame maps only its 27,340,800-byte plane-0 prefix into one slot; the CPU decode remains the fallback when the display-bank window is unavailable.
- gpucomp26 log \(recovery, userdata window, t=16..917 s\): UKE DPU: direct ready fmt=000237ff/000237ff unpack=03020001 tenbit=000276ff; four b3a8 frames mapped by their plane-0 prefix \(direct slot ... bytes=27340800\); UKE DPU: tenbit sample p0=18060180 p0c=37edfb7e alpha=ff/ff, so the alpha plane is 0xFF, the frame is opaque and the XRGB2101010 word order is the right one. One stall snapshot at t=78.4 s and none after; GPU commit-to-complete still averages 50.9 ms over 8884 commits in 893 s \(about 9.9/s\).
- gpucomp27 ports the upstream display's refresh tick \(GopFramebuffer 33e941a\): startVsync/vsyncTick/armVsync, getRefreshAttributes at slot 301 and setDisplayRefreshProperties in registerService, with the builder binding \_native\_legacy\_vsync\_notify \(0xfffffff005796720\) and \_native\_legacy\_set\_refresh\_properties \(0xfffffff00579ebb8\) after checking the original bytes; board.toml sets sheng-vsync=120 \(armcpu-diagnostics dropped to stay at 987 of 1000 command-line bytes\). The direct path now prints UKE DPU: flip n=... tb=... skip=... fb=... latch=...us max=...us vsync=... every 120 frames and the first eight UKE DPU: skip lines, so the next log separates compositor starvation from display-path loss.
- gpucomp27 counters: flips ran at 10-28 fps with skip=0 and fb=0, so the display path drops nothing; the latch waits for the frame boundary \(mean 4.1 ms, max 8.4 ms, one 120 Hz frame\) and the flip is tear-free. The compositor commits every 85-100 ms when idle and 12-30 ms in bursts, one full-screen pass with 60 draws over 6.68 Mpx per frame, commit-to-complete 27-41 ms; the GMU perf vote is accepted \(X v0/0, index 9 = 950 MHz, bandwidth 6\), so the frame cost is fill work, not clock.
- Flicker root cause found in the display code: dpuSlot rotated the four slots round-robin and could pick the slot the pipes were scanning, remapping that IOVA to a new surface while the pipe still fetched the old frame, one mixed frame per burst of new surfaces \(app transitions, blur effects\). gpucomp28 selects by LRU and never the shown slot \(upstream directSlot rule\) and records it after each flip.
- gpucomp28 also removes the one-frame delay: CoreAnimation completes its command buffer before submitting the swap \(the runtime WC row is its waitUntilCompleted, 29 calls, 738 ms\), and upstream flips the submitted surface; the delay only added up to a whole frame of display latency \(about 100 ms at 10 fps\).
- Open on the GPU side: SR 5 refusals of 3200x2136 surface textures \(pf80 BGRA / pf554 ten-bit\) with OutOfMemory\(Device\), and 220 GB of shadow copy-backs \(J surface cache\) over a 320 s run; both may add cost to the compositor path and need a separate look.
- gpucomp29 counted 22 drained and 1298 busy flips: the GPU ring was non-empty in 98 percent of flips, so holding each frame one swap longer did not mean holding a complete one \(the compositor submits before its work retires\). That is why transitions and blur effects kept flickering and why the first seconds after boot flashed black.
- gpucomp30 gives the direct path a completion fence: dpuPrepareFrame does the sleepable work \(slot mapping, source format\) at swap time, a work-loop timer polls native\_gpu\_idle \(the CP ring drained\) every 500 us and only then writes the registers \(dpuFlipPrepared\), forcing the flip after 30 ms and counting it \(fence=drained/forced\) so a ring that never drains cannot freeze the panel; dpuSlot now avoids both the shown and the pending slot and a newer frame replaces one still waiting.
- The CPU levels are logged again without a new boot argument: CpuFreqProbe runs its read-only mode on the GPU track \(sheng-gpu-power&gt;=9\), reports the level each EPSS domain runs at \(f&lt;MHz&gt;, LUT\[perf\_state\]\) and logs UKE CPU: ...; the windows are 0x17d91000/0x17d92000/0x17d93000, referenced by the vendor crash-dump link lists.
- gpucomp30 showed that CP\_RB\_RPTR==CP\_RB\_WPTR is not a completion signal \(the CP has read the commands, the pixel work may still run\), so that fence behaved like the unfenced gpucomp28: everything that moved flickered.
- gpucomp31 takes the fence from the only party that knows: the plugin Metal runtime. NativeGOPWire gains NGP\_DIRECT \('DIRC'\), the presenter offers it when sheng-fb-gpu=1 and uke-dpu-scanout&gt;=3, the client waits in NBWaitInFlight for every command buffer committed before the offer \(exactly this frame's work, the earlyOn gate removed\) and completes, and the display flips its pipes in that completion \(completeDirect\) with a 100 ms watchdog that drops an unanswered frame; clients without the mode are refused and the display keeps its own timing.
- The plugin had to be rebuilt for the direct mode \(CDHashes c063675e/4be6b25b/ee022a5f, owner trust regenerated, addon blob 21,178,648 bytes, 12 entries\). On Linux the plugin build must not pass -fuse-ld=lld: the iOS xcrun wrapper already injects --ld-path to its ld shim, and the two together hand clang driver flags to ld64.lld.
- gpucomp37 measured the GPU path itself: a 27 MB fill takes 14.8 ms \(1.76 GB/s\) and a copy 13.9 ms \(3.75 GB/s\), so the compositor's \~67 MB of shadow traffic per frame is about 18 ms before any rendering. The bottleneck is GPU-to-host memory throughput, not shading.
- Upstream was fetched with its feature branches: NativeAdreno feat/ipados27-gpu-parity \(14 commits, 2026-10-29 02:39\) carries the work that targets this exactly - cache maintenance counted per phase with uncached imports skipped \(its message: the 1.5 GiB pool took 41 ms with preemption off\), no XNU contiguous search when the pool exists, BO pool growth in chunks, on-demand GPU page tables, msm hangcheck with restart. ArmSmmu4K has the matching feat branch \(30ec63b, page tables on demand\).
- gpucomp38 is those branches merged: our nine A732/GMU commits rebased onto the adreno feat branch with the conflicts resolved \(borrow and adopt side by side, the GMU pool adopt taking only the five payloads because page tables are on demand now, the GPU pool adopt for the five buffers, at\(i\) for the grown entry table, dropViews split into a locked form and a wrapper\), plus a weak native\_pmap\_user\_mappings stub and a UKE GPU: maint phase row. The SMMU kit is the feat commit; the build needs --local-module kit:ArmSmmu4K= that checkout.
- Local toolchain fixes on the way: build-arm-driver.py dropped the recipe's -Wno-missing-designated-field-initializers and -Wno-unused-but-set-global, which made clang reject the XNU headers and then assert inside RecordLayoutBuilder \(the earlier 'clang frontend command failed due to signal'\); both are back, and a compile that dies by signal is retried.
- The upstream feature branch was merged and boots: gpucomp46 \(kernel 15ad83d8, payload 48967bf0\) ran 918 s with conn switch=0 cp=0 mode=1 mount=0/0 shared=0 tables=10/1 in=31 out=40, o2/0/1 and fence=4892/28 \(99.4 percent fenced, drops down from 11-12 percent\), gpu=12.1 ms offer-to-completion. The per-process client address space works on the fused 31-bit aperture because clientWindow\(\) places every client at 0x10000000 + 1.75 GiB \(its own page tables make the same window free for all of them\); createVm, setScope and the client opens then all succeed.
- A later boot of the same image panicked with OSMetaClass::applyToInstances+0xf4 \(caller OSKext::loadExecutable, pc 0xfffffff007904548, esr 0x56000004 SVC, x8 NativeLegacyDisplay::getMetaClass\); the string resolves to '&gt;maxDepth' at libkern/c++/OSMetaClass.cpp:1118, i.e. a metaclass superclass chain deeper than sDeepestClass. That is kernel data being overwritten, and it appeared only after the feature branch's per-process address spaces and on-demand page tables, intermittently \(one boot 918 s clean, the next panics during kext load\).
- The gpu\_qcom\_scm aperture call \(SVC\_MP 0x0c / cmd 0x1b, ARGS\(4\)\) succeeds for context banks 0-3 but the SMMU keeps reporting a 31-bit stage-1 input \(in=31\), so the upstream 2^ias model cannot be used here; the client window is what adapts instead.
- Panic capture: the pram hand-off \(uke\_panic\_flush\) produced no UKE PANIC line and a variadic call to the kernel printf printed only the prefix \(Darwin passes variadic arguments on the stack\), so the panic screen now copies its text into uke\_panic\_shadow \(exported by the probe\) and the probe heartbeat prints it; the screen also mutes the console on entry and draws only the message at scale 3.
- The revert to the bootable baseline exposed how a local build picks its driver: feeds.toml's Adreno740 commit \(f1a2aee, the m9f-era tip\) is documentation only for a local= source, and the build stages whatever that checkout has. gpucomp47/48 therefore built with f1a2aee and rendered on the CPU, while the image that rendered on the GPU was gpucomp37, built from uke-a732-single @ c950906 \(the local A732 commits rebased onto upstream perf/gpu-smoothness\). The two payloads' kernel.macho symbol tables settle it: gpucomp37 has retiredReaper, NativeAdrenoRing::kick/writeWptr, native\_adreno\_stall\*/ring\_window/wptr\_fence/unstick, AGXDeviceUserClient::dropViewsLocked and NativeAdrenoHandleIndex; f1a2aee has none of them. gpucomp49 \(driver c950906, plus the probe's pool\_mib write before bring-up and the &\#123;1,9,6&\#125; initial HFI vote that the same commit had\) has exactly gpucomp37's kernel size \(52,281,344 B\) and differs only in \_\_LINKEDIT \(216 B\) and the six omitted feat telemetry symbols; user-verified on device on 2026-10-29.
- 2026-10-30 \(branch uke-b3a8-scanout, GopFramebuffer 4458e4d, image gpucomp72, built not flashed\): the upstream feat display refuses the render server's two-plane frame, so the spelling joins nativeScanoutFormat with its own 5-bytes-a-texel bound \(native\_legacy\_b3a8\_bounds\) instead of the 4 B/px model its pixel-unit stride can never satisfy; plane 0 is scanned through the same VIG pipes with the ten-bit source word \(\(firmware word &amp; \~0x100\) \| 0x4000 = 0x000276ff, unpack 0x03020001\) and only that plane's 27,340,800-byte prefix mapped, and native\_legacy\_convert\_xr10 decodes it on the CPU when the pipes are not reachable. The direct path also needed the display bank window back: its argument is NativeUfs's uke-dpu-scanout, which the 2026-10-30 02:16 move to the upstream chain dropped while keeping only the display's sheng-dpu-scanout, so from gpucomp50 on every frame took the copy path; board.toml now sets both \(sheng-dpu-scanout=2 uke-dpu-scanout=1\) at 974 of 1000 command-line bytes, dropping offline-legacy-base-present, which only the retired NativeMobileDisplay read.
- 2026-11-10 input/backlight: reused sheng maina68ee4ee and core BacklightMode54796a7, adapted uke PON/GPIO6 and dual KTZ8866. ASan/UBSan5082 host checks and owner runtime signature/original-section/negative-page checks passed. Formal kernel/payload/13-area/FAT/signature/UEFI-roundtrip checks passed; native2x candidate A79E83AEA028FE78/vendor\_boot9ad565a1a01a529c61f4b7baffd256ca301725963d3ce54fe58572456ac7d17b is unflashed. Display225 companion inherits prior display failures and is DO\_NOT\_FLASH; no hardware verification or device operations. Existing device-verified status belongs to earlier accepted display functionality, not new buttons/backlight.

### 完成条件

- A known frame appears with correct geometry and colors and stays up.

### 当前范围

- Measured luminance, repeated direct screen-off/wake transitions, physical-button stress and SoC deep suspend have separate coverage.

### 下一步

- Extend low/middle/high brightness and repeated screen-off/wake coverage. Keep measured display cadence and transition timing separate.

## 输入 {#input}

`device-verified`

组件: `ShengHidInput`, `ShengNovatekTouch`, `QcomGeniSpi`.

### 硬件与依赖

- Novatek NT36532 \(o82 firmware, csot and tm variants\) over Qcom Geni SPI.
- HID delivery through the workloop \(NativeHidInput runAction on the touch thread\).

### 配置

- 60x40 scan geometry; TLMM per-GPIO base 0x0f100000; poll mode frames flow after first valid frame type 4.
- Kernel arguments offline-hid-home=1 offline-hid-input=1 offline-key-events=1.

### 操作步骤

- Boot with the touch diagnostics and read the UKE TCH counters.

### 开发经验

- 2026-11-09 upstream sheng pulled --ff-only to a68ee4ee67cc71792fbf519de7e3e28072c717b5. ShengHidInput main71a40e76daeae1bb4127546f96ffa7339c7a06ff has identical tracked content to24f1f3fbed6feff42e1ca02e2ce386a5c3c36da8. Pin synchronized without claiming gesture repair; raw axes30480x20320 preserved, no display-pixel substitution. Dock and Stage Manager require device results.
- 2026-11-03 ca-surface-track: user explicitly confirms normal upward-swipe entry without the previous freeze; CRC0485290E96ACC9B3/final988d162c/vendor150b5c06. Evidence is user report, not only flash or host checks.
- Full bring-up runs on device through stage 4: firmware download CRC-ok and scanning 60x40.
- The v3 argument set is sheng-fb-gpu=0 plus offline-legacy-base-present=1; the desktop-era fb-gpu=1 hangs identically.
- 2026-11-10 input/backlight: reused sheng maina68ee4ee and core BacklightMode54796a7, adapted uke PON/GPIO6 and dual KTZ8866. ASan/UBSan5082 host checks and owner runtime signature/original-section/negative-page checks passed. Formal kernel/payload/13-area/FAT/signature/UEFI-roundtrip checks passed; native2x candidate A79E83AEA028FE78/vendor\_boot9ad565a1a01a529c61f4b7baffd256ca301725963d3ce54fe58572456ac7d17b is unflashed. Display225 companion inherits prior display failures and is DO\_NOT\_FLASH; no hardware verification or device operations. Existing device-verified status belongs to earlier accepted input functionality, not new buttons/backlight.

### 完成条件

- Frames reach HID and the on-screen swipe responds.

### 当前范围

- Current 27-line acceptance is user-confirmed upward swipe without freeze; duration, all-app coverage, cross-reboot stability and GPU acceleration are outside this report.
- Split touch HID delivery and visible swipe are now user-confirmed; the former weak-stub/no-delivery limit is superseded. Multi-touch, full direction coverage and latency were not measured in this report.
- Power/volume physical keys, CoreBrightness slider and screen-off/on backlight restoration are hardware-unverified; SoC deep suspend remains out of scope.

### 下一步

- After per-candidate authorization, validate 129116FF4B98EE39 builtin service LockKeyDelay0 and short presses; collect IRQ/inbox/deadline flow counters before claiming IRQ latency fixed. Preserve 065F rollback and test 20 off/on cycles.

## GPU {#gpu}

`device-verified`

组件: `Adreno740`, `AdrenoMetal`, `AdrenoFirmware`, `AdrenoPM4`, `ArmSmmu4K`, `Sm7675Platform`, `Sm8550Platform`, `AirNir`.

### 硬件与依赖

- Real 0x43030b00/gen7\_11\_0, pinned gen71100 firmware, OEM DT and OEM gen7\_11\_0 source ed4ad0d1ad00.
- Upstream main Adreno740 6fa7bae, Metal 243d63c, GOP e47c1b8; existing 24A446 OS/SystemOS and immutable Data seed.

### 配置

- NATIVE\_ADRENO enabled; per-process VM, preemption mode2, DCVS, BGRA8 scanout, Turnip render-mode selection and concurrent binning.
- 160 MiB raw FAT with standard UEFI compression; paired uke UEFI required.

### 操作步骤

- Build kernel, UEFI, Turnip ios27, Metal ios27, addon/trust and payload; pack boot/vendor\_boot.
- Only the compression round trip and required artifact/record checks; hardware tests follow delivery.

### 开发经验

- 2026-11-15: The user reports no further GPU freezes in current use. Earlier Safari, App Library and desktop failures apply to their historical images.
- 2026-11-13 f33ab396 user-confirmed deployment froze after Safari webpage loaded. Same-boot92E994D1:122.609s switch timeout replayed6 successfully; GPU/display/Data advanced to219.405637s. Final frozen state absent; high-load GPU cause unproven.
- 2026-11-12 d7b39501 device: icons and wallpaper normal. Three backboardd Submit calls returned BadArgument and led to SIGTERM/respring. Separate ring-switch timeout at173s and ring0 SMMU translation fault at210s both recovered. PID525 continued hardware rendering.
- 2026-11-12 replay candidate38703fa6 failed: missing desktop icons/wallpaper and black screen. Same-boot01D32BCB shows iconservicesagent MadeBeforeRecovery at99.61s, backboardd SIGSEGV at136.993s in Turnip CmdBindVertexBuffers2 writing NULL, and replacement GPU VM attach failing ArmSmmu4KFault step0xb30. Original fault remains unresolved.
- Real gen7\_11\_0 GPU stack preserves upstream features and uses original OS resources. Turnip chained fixups avoids prior dyld stack overflow; scoped CoreImage nil-result library retry is present.
- On CRC90B6BC701210D9DB the user confirms all previously reported boot/icon/widget/Settings/Dock UI-restart symptoms resolved. Device result, not just flash transport.

### 完成条件

- Real Metal rendering/presentation and unlock work on uke 24A446.
- Concurrent clients, CP preemption, teardown and persistent warm shader cache work on hardware.

### 当前范围

- The current user observation covers normal use. Exact presentation cadence, repeated cold/warm cache behavior, sustained multi-client stress and fault recovery remain separate hardware checks.

### 下一步

- Extend long-run rendering and fault recovery coverage. On recurrence collect same-boot GPU fences, display latches and CPU progress.

No extra long-run/frame-time/reboot-cache coverage inferred.](https://github.com/Allenwdk/UniXNU-uke/blob/215a7880d4469fefa2a5286045dd63b81e7c7200/docs/GPU-IOS27.md)
  Command: <code>User hardware feedback after flash-result-20261103-223133.json; record user-observation-success.json</code>. Revision: `10a077a8b4de9b8333069089e808ab8ad2cba8bd`.

## 网络 {#network}

`device-verified`

组件: `Sm7675Platform`, `Ath12kWifi`.

### 硬件与依赖

- Stock WCN6750 signed WPSS pieces from the read-only modem\_a firmware partition, pinned in firmware/wcn6750/manifest.json.
- Uke live DTS: WPSS25MiB at0x9bb00000, MSA12MiB at0xa6400000, SMEM GLINK/IPCRTR remotePID13 and IPCC client24; PAS6.
- DMA ownership \(porting/platform\): apps\_smmu@15000000 stream SID0x1400 mask1, IOVA pool0xb0000000+0x10000000, MSI doorbell0x17110040 and SPI0x300-0x31f, dma-coherent; BAR0x61e00000+0x200000 from DEVICE\_INFO.
- Official layering target \(porting/network\): nw\_abi lx\_platform/nw\_board -&gt; nw\_wifi worker -&gt; WifiProbe hardware -&gt; WifiNet OS. Ath12kWifi contains no WCN6750 board; the ath12k PCIe/MHI worker cannot be selected unchanged.

### 配置

- WPSS control worker is linked through the 24A446 Legacy board archive; uke-wifi defaults to mode3 \(ath11k AHB data path\) with explicit uke-wifi=0/1/2 override. Existing999-byte boot line and GPU addon are preserved.
- The Legacy archive links the pinned Linux QMI codec and original ath11k CAP/DEVICE\_INFO descriptors. The board owns the QRTR client port and validates the response bounds before decoding.
- Shared CX/MX maximum cmd-db votes and XO are retained by this diagnostic. Runtime vote ownership, WifiNet/Apple80211 and the userspace supplicant remain pending.
- The stack runs under the module's own lifecycle: nw\_wifi.c is built with NW\_PLATFORM\_AHB \(PCI/MHI/QRTR-MHI and the ath12k diagnostics are compiled out\), nw\_ahb.c supplies the platform object model, and nw\_diag\_ahb.c provides nw\_wifi\_ring\_state/rx\_state/tx\_reasons/link\_state from ath11k structures. The board only fills struct nw\_board, calls nw\_wifi\_start/step and reads nw\_wifi\_ring\_state.
- org.unixnu.wifi.control is registered as a privileged extended kernel control \(ctl\_sendsize 256KiB, ctl\_recvsize 512KiB\) with connect/disconnect/send/getopt/rcvd callbacks ported from sheng; the transport's send uses ctl\_enqueuedata and wake wakes the WPSS worker. A single self-test session issues NW\_CONTROL\_INFO so the queue path is proven before userspace exists.
- The OS network side is sheng's WifiNet.cpp kept byte-identical: it publishes en0 and the Apple80211 ioctls, installs nw\_wifi\_events and owns RX/TX. Its IO80211/IONetwork classes register late through ukeClassBegin/ukeClassEnd around \_\_GLOBAL\_\_sub\_I\_WifiNet.cpp, driven from the board once nw\_wifi\_stats.stage reaches 4. The ADT that make-uke-adt.py generates now carries /arm-io/wlan \(compatible qcom,wcn6750, local-mac-address 02:00:00:75:67:50\) for 24A446's wifid, and patches/xnu-12377/uke-wifi-association-event.patch lets kev\_post\_msg carry the 540-byte association result in a cluster mbuf \(Apple IEEE80211 class only\).
- ath11k debug output is the driver's own: ath11k\_debug\_mask defaults to 0x76 \(WMI\|HTC\|MAC\|BOOT\|QMI\) and the boot argument ath11k-debug-mask=&lt;n&gt; overrides it. The shim lx\_log also expands the %s-prefixed va\_format shape \_\_ath11k\_dbg uses, so debug lines keep their mask name instead of printing a pointer. No board-side WMI/HTC trace is added.

### 操作步骤

- ./unixnu build kernel &amp;&amp; ./unixnu build payload
- python3 ../UniXNU-uke-build/scripts/check-uke-wifi-firmware.py --protocol-dir ../UniXNU-sm7675-common/kits/Sm7675Platform/remoteproc
- Official order: prove platform interrupts and DMA ownership first, then lx\_platform/nw\_board callbacks, then the worker, then the OS interface. WCN6750 follows the same layers with an ath11k AHB/hybrid backend.
- After current flash authorization, inspect fresh UKE WIFI stage/ready/IPCRTR/WLFW and board-data logs before adding the DMA/MSI path.

### 开发经验

- 2026-11-15: The user confirms Wi-Fi works in current use. The V4.9.7 baseline already passed the Settings row, open and password-protected association, DHCP, and app/Safari traffic.
- 2026-11-09 identity/touch branch changes only HID feed provenance; HID tracked code and Wi-Fi/GPU runtime sources unchanged. V4.9.7 main baseline preserved. No new association,traffic,lifecycle or device acceptance inferred.
- 2026-11-08 V4.9.3 host repair: pollGl now calls nw\_wifi\_poll on each worker/nested pump as sheng6894001 pPoll does. Level7 per-channel/per-buffer debug filtered and recent16 debug hashes deduplicated; warnings/errors and native control diagnostics retained. CTLWORK v493 counts poll/gapmax/stepmax/logdrop. A single focused ASan/UBSan fixture compiles the production board callback slice with real nw\_control/lx\_rt: missing-poll control exceeds3000ms without dispatch, fixed reply95ms, mutex-held work deferred until unlock, MSI/GLINK and repeated errors preserved. These timings are simulated, not device measurements. Formal kernel and payload pass; runtime source delta vs V4.9.2 is only kits/uke/WifiAth11k.cpp SHA e9160fbb7e113041961839548a6ddadcd5ee37c6a3b684e22ddc344cb86c6501. No userspace/WPA/AMFI/trust/firmware/DMA changes. Networking and Settings remain unverified; no flash or host userdata write. Workspace report docs/status/wifi-v49-3-worker-poll-2026-11-08.md.
- 2026-11-08 V4 device readback: everything up to the supplicant works - UKE WIFI BIN stage=5 status=0 \(the addon binary is mounted at /usr/libexec\), the control self-test passes, wifid\[50\] runs, and a join arrives as layout 656 with key length 0 cipher 0 \(an open join, because the supplicant never attached\). The supplicant does start: launchd spawns it \(PIDs 44/103/108/116/131/147/183/193/204/219\) and every spawn is refused by AMFI with 'Launch Constraint Violation \(enforcing\), error info: c\[8\]p\[1\]m\[1\]e\[6\], \(Constraint not matched\)' for /usr/libexec/unixnu-wpa\_supplicant, launch type 1. Six corpses, no control session. iOS 27 enforces launch constraints on system-service launches and an ad-hoc signature carries none. The AMFI kext in the kernelcache exposes amfi\_enforce\_launch\_constraints and amfi\_allow\_3p\_launch\_constraints, so the narrow switch is amfi\_enforce\_launch\_constraints=0 rather than the global amfi=0x80. Boot arguments were at 999 bytes, so sheng-diag=4, sheng-gpu-trace=2 and -enable\_kprintf\_spam were dropped to land at 981 bytes.
- 2026-11-08 V4: the native wpa\_supplicant cannot be built on the Linux host \(the iPhoneOS userland SDK, xcrun and codesign only exist on macOS; tools/build-supplicant.py --target ios stops at a missing stdlib.h\). It is now built by a dispatch-only macOS workflow in the device repo \(.github/workflows/build-wifi-supplicant.yml\) from UniXNU/Ath12kWifi at 28de7b9, ad-hoc signed with identifier org.unixnu.wifi.supplicant, and uploaded with its CDHash. The Linux side computes the same CDHash from the Mach-O \(LC\_CODE\_SIGNATURE -&gt; CodeDirectory -&gt; sha256\[:20\]\) because this host's codesign port verifies signatures but prints nothing for --display; the CI value 2941c69fcc5c43148546935e5cebc0f03ce9cf4b matches the parsed one.
- 2026-11-08 V4 packaging: build-wifi-userspace.py stages bin/etc/wifi/daemons without firmware \(the driver reads the stock modem partition\); the supplicant job is merged into the icon-service launchd cache \(716 jobs, icon environment intact, one added owner job, detached signature regenerated\) so no second /System/Library/xpc mount is needed; uke-wifi-launchd-cache.patch adds only the /usr/libexec union mount \(scratch 0xc00, launchd\_cache\_loader stays visible\); the addon gains wifi-bin/unixnu-wpa\_supplicant + etc/wifi + the new xpc cache, and trust-append-module.py adds the supplicant and cache CDHashes \(4 -&gt; 5 modules\). unixnu.local.toml now points gpu\_addon/owner\_trust\_cache at the fixes directory inputs.
- 2026-11-08 V3.1 device result: the missing piece was wifid's launch condition, not the driver. Its launchd job \(/System/Library/LaunchDaemons/com.apple.wifid.plist in the shipped cache: RunAtLoad, KeepAlive, Program /usr/sbin/wifid\) carries \_LimitLoadToDeviceTree = \[wlan, marconi-wifi, centauri-alpha, centauri-alpha-protium\], and it is the only Wi-Fi daemon with that key. The check runs against the device tree the boot image supplies; sheng's \[bootimg\] dtb comes from sheng.dts, whose root level carries wlan, while uke.dts had no such node \(V3 had only added the ADT /arm-io/wlan\). Adding the root-level node from sheng verbatim \(compatible qcom,wcn7850, status okay\) plus an ADT mirror made /usr/sbin/wifid\[49\] start, and the user confirmed Control Center shows Wi-Fi and scans real networks. The readback covers kCardCaps\(34\), kPower\(25\), kScanReq\(3 OK\), kScanResult\(42\), kCurrentNetwork\(144, ENOENT while unassociated\), kSupportedChannels, kHwSupportedChannels\(19\), kCountryCode\(7\); newer types 25/96/97/106/115/207/365/389/390/423/473/481/485/512/560/564/566/571/572/575/582 answer EOPNOTSUPP.
- 2026-11-08 V3.1 Settings surface: Preferences\[398\] started and loaded no Wi-Fi panel extension at all \(no WiFiSettingsControlsExtension/WiFiKit/MobileWiFi line\), so the absent Settings row is not a disable switch: the sheng-lite list \(BootWatchdogPolicy.c\) contains no Wi-Fi daemon, sheng-disable-jobs is not used, the shipped launchd cache keeps all 715 stock jobs, and wifid's DT gate is now satisfied. The only OS-level complaint is Protobox: wifid\(49\) deny\(1\) iokit-open-service IO80211Interface, which the WifiNet design \(sheng verbatim\) expects: it serves daemons on the ioctl path and answers newUserClient with kIOReturnUnsupported. Whether the Settings pane needs a real IO80211InterfaceUserClient has no log evidence yet and is a separate diagnosis.
- 2026-11-08 V3 adds the OS layer. WifiNet.cpp is sheng's file byte-identical \(52,547 B\) with only its three board services replaced: native\_wifi\_logf prints through the board, sheng\_wifi\_file answers ENOENT \(this device has no join.txt source, so autoJoin disables itself\), sheng\_wifi\_wake is the AHB worker wake. Registration follows the established uke late-class pattern \(globalise \_\_GLOBAL\_\_sub\_I\_WifiNet.cpp, drop \_\_DATA,\_\_mod\_init\_func, run it inside ukeClassBegin/ukeClassEnd\), triggered from the workstation loop once nw\_wifi\_stats.stage reaches 4 so the MAC is already published. Two more keep-list entries were required \(nw\_wifi\_buffer\_stats\_get, nw\_wifi\_power\): WifiNet lives in the same archive and objcopy renames every non-kept global.
- 2026-11-08 V3 OS prerequisites: make-uke-adt.py now emits /arm-io/wlan \(qcom,wcn6750, local-mac-address\) because 24A446's wifid matches that node; the build's adt\_sha256 moves from 613a3486 to 74000706, which the packaging asserts. patches/xnu-12377/uke-wifi-association-event.patch is sheng's 33-line change to kev\_post\_msg verbatim: for KEV\_VENDOR\_APPLE + KEV\_IEEE80211\_CLASS the limit becomes MCLBYTES and the message uses m\_getcl, so the 540-byte association result \(fields at 0x214/0x218\) fits. It applies with -390 lines of offset to this tree; every other class keeps MLEN.
- 2026-11-08 V2 puts the stack under the module's own lifecycle. nw\_wifi.c gains an NW\_PLATFORM\_AHB branch that skips ath12k\_wifi7\_pci\_init/mhi\_init\_hook/nw\_qrtr\_mhi\_register/pci\_state/wmi\_stuck/log\_tap and the ath12k-only globals \(nw\_wifi\_stats and nw\_wifi\_hw\_ready stay with nw\_mainline.c, which owns them in this image\), and starts through nw\_ahb\_start\(\)/nw\_ahb\_stop\(\). nw\_wifi\_step/poll/wait\_ms/irq\_mode/irq\_pending are unchanged, so nw\_wifi\_control\_poll\(\) is now on the worker path for the first time. The four diagnostics move out of the board into src/nw\_diag\_ahb.c, implemented against ath11k structures \(hal srng free counts, reo\_dst/tx completion rings, station status\); nw\_wifi\_link\_state carries no rate because this tree has no ath12k\_dp\_link\_peer\_get\_sta\_rate\_info\_stats equivalent \(recorded deviation\). Board WifiAth11kState.c is retired.
- 2026-11-08 V2 board boundary: the board fills struct nw\_board \(bar from nw\_board\_bar\(\), irq\_base 0x400, map\_phys, no cfg space\), installs lx\_platform, registers the control socket, then calls nw\_wifi\_start\(&amp;gPlatform, &amp;board\); the WPSS worker loop calls nw\_wifi\_step\(\) \(which owns lx\_pump, so the board no longer pumps separately\) and prints nw\_wifi\_ring\_state every 5 s. MSI bitmaps go through nw\_wifi\_irq\_pending\(\), ring inventory through the module. Every symbol the board references must be in the rename keep list: the first link failed on nw\_board, nw\_wifi\_set\_platform, nw\_control\_begin and nw\_control\_decode after objcopy renamed them to \_nwk\*.
- 2026-11-08 device D61F closes the RX-assertion phase: PMK8550 GPIO3 was still an unwritten plain GPIO \(mode00 vin00 pull04 source00 enable80\) and after the board wrote func1/no-bias/VIN0/output-enable \(mode01 pull05 source02 enable80\) the firmware completed a whole scan, scan done reason0 at93.459782s, BSS snapshot20. SPI mask0001002f, DP group6 served by IRQ1040 with NAPI, and no whalRxMpduPop or whalRecvDmaLeak assertion through364.846024s. The refill ring TP=2110 that earlier candidates treated as exhaustion is also present in this successful scan and is not by itself a fault indicator; neither is the shared MAC129 HP=0.
- 2026-11-08 P0 firmware dispatch: the board fwGet answered every non-regdb name with bd\_o82.elf \(59952B\), so ath11k's API-2 board-2.bin container parsed an ELF. The D61F log shows board-2.bin requested three times at51.630-52.545s and rejected each time with 'found invalid board magic', then board.bin answered 59952B and the stack logged 'boot using board api 1'; amss.bin and m3.bin are never requested because WPSS PAS loads the firmware from modem\_a through modem:qca6750/wpss. The dispatch now compares the basename and answers only board.bin \(BDF type1\) and regdb.bin/regdb\_xiaomi.bin \(REGDB type4\), returning ENOENT for everything else and logging one 'fw request &lt;name&gt; -&gt; &lt;answer&gt;' line per distinct name so the actual request set stays visible.
- 2026-11-08 preemption callbacks: \_disable\_preemption/\_enable\_preemption are out-of-line global symbols in the 24A446 kernel image at 0xfffffff00af21308 and 0xfffffff00af2144c \(llvm-nm on kernel-final.macho\), so the board binds the kernel pair instead of the previous empty callbacks and the shared lx\_shared\_spin\_lock boundary is no longer preemptible between the atomic and the payload copy. The board object imports both symbols and the link resolves them; lx\_spin\_enter/leave pair per acquisition and lx\_shared\_spin\_trylock leaves on failure, so the counter cannot underflow.
- 2026-11-07 uke board DMA: reuse sheng WifiProbe allocation and coalescing in the existing 48MiB uncached 32-bit IOVA window. Refuse an absent uncached alias and out-of-window mappings; require all 32 hardware MSI vectors and report actual SPI counts. Linux and the OEM WCN6750 entry both use rxdma1\_enable=false. This corrects the board DMA contract; the whalRxMpduPop firmware assertion still needs a device retest.
- 2026-11-06 MSI delivery root cause: the firmware's doorbell write was already landing in the polled sink page \(24 records of 0x322 from 71.463s in boot-20261106-201200\), but pollMsiSink was only called next to lx\_pump in the board's main loop, while ath11k's entire core\_start \(htc.c:534 htc\_wait\_target, htc.c:672 connect\_service\) is synchronous and only yields through lx\_pump -&gt; lx\_platform.poll, which then moved GLINK only. A firmware MSI therefore could not be observed or dispatched during start, so complete\(&amp;htc-&gt;ctl\_resp\) never ran and the wait timed out with -110. 0x322 = ep\_base\_data 0x320 + vector 2, and the WCN6750 service map puts HTC \(RSVD\_CTRL\) and WMI\_CONTROL target-&gt;host on CE2 with ath11k\_htc\_rx\_completion\_handler. Independently, the AHB hybrid hif ops install no get\_ce\_msi\_idx \(only pci.c:916 does\), so hif.h falls back to msi\_data\_idx = ce\_id: firmware data is 0x320 + ce\_id while the host registers at compacted indices; CE0-CE3 agree and CE4/CE6 send no MSI, which covers the whole bring-up path.
- 2026-11-07 debug-mask readback of 1497FEF9FCB11D48 opens the whole WMI sequence for the first time: WMI\_INIT, then pdev set param 34/10/35/143/1/2, SCAN\_PROB\_REQ\_OUI, two SCAN\_CHAN\_LIST commands \(72 and 30 channels\), LRO\_CONFIG, pdev set param 44 \(IDLE\_PS\_CONFIG=1\) and finally VDEV\_CREATE. Every one of them is consumed by the firmware on the same \~11 ms credit round trip, and VDEV\_CREATE is followed by an MSI on vector 3 \(CE3, the host-&gt;target WMI source ring\). After that there are zero credit returns, zero MSIs on any vector, 1054 insufficient-credit lines, four WMI timeouts and the WPSS assert. This confirms the credit mechanism, the CE3 doorbell and the static window arithmetic are all correct and localises the stall to the firmware handling of vdev create. The crash record is truncated to 40 characters; the firmware string table in wpss.b06 carries whal\_wmac\_transition\_to\_fw\_control as the only name with that prefix, and its neighbours are whal\_wmac\_phy\_off\_check, whal\_wmac\_h2s\_grant\_check \(Polling H2S\_GRANT\_ISSUED failed\) and the IMPS/HW-DTIM enter paths, i.e. the MAC low power recipe rather than the WMI parser. The host enables exactly that recipe 110 ms earlier with WMI\_PDEV\_PARAM\_IDLE\_PS\_CONFIG from ath11k\_mac\_op\_start, before any interface exists. The previous mbssid hypothesis is refuted: the firmware advertises wmi\_ext2\_service\_\_bitmap\[3\] = 0x400020, i.e. bit 253 WMI\_TLV\_SERVICE\_MBSS\_PARAM\_IN\_VDEV\_START\_SUPPORT, so it claims to accept the 8 extra bytes.
- 2026-11-07 upstream cross-check of the WMI stall \(read-only, mainline v7.0.14 vs OEM 6.1.68\), no behaviour changed: \(a\) the WCN6750 static window arithmetic is correct for this board -- HAL\_SEQ\_WCSS\_UMAC\_OFFSET 0x00a00000 and hal\_seq\_wcss\_umac\_ce0\_src\_reg 0x01b80000 both mask to 0 under ATH11K\_PCI\_WINDOW\_RANGE\_MASK 0x7ffff, so DP lands in \[0x80000,0xfffff\] and CE in \[0x100000,0x17ffff\], inside the 2 MiB BAR that DEVICE\_INFO reports \(0x61e00000 size 0x200000\) and disjoint from each other; the shadow area 0x504+36\*4 is below WINDOW\_START and takes the direct path. The suspected HP mis-write is therefore excluded. \(b\) Re-reading boot-20261107-022026 in time order shows the WMI\_VDEV\_CREATE\_CMDID DID consume a credit and WAS read by the firmware: msi vector 3 \(CE3, the host-&gt;target WMI source ring\) fires at 59.132 s, one line after the last of the 13 credit returns at 59.125 s, and no failed-to-create-vdev line exists, while the very next command \(the first WMI\_VDEV\_SET\_PARAM\_CMDID, WMI\_VDEV\_PARAM\_TX\_ENCAP\_TYPE\) never gets a credit. The credit flow therefore stops while the firmware handles vdev create, not at the first command and not because of the source-ring offset. \(c\) The only wire-format divergence found between our tree and the OEM 6.1.68 kernel in that command path is exactly the command that stalls: wmi\_vdev\_create\_cmd is 10 u32 \(TLV\_LEN 36\) upstream and 8 u32 \(TLV\_LEN 28\) in OEM because of mbssid\_flags/mbssid\_tx\_vdev\_id. Every other structure used before the stall \(wmi\_pdev\_set\_param\_cmd, wmi\_vdev\_set\_param\_cmd, wmi\_scan\_chan\_list\_cmd, wmi\_pdev\_set\_ps\_mode\_cmd\) is field-identical, the sampled wmi\_tlv\_cmd\_id/wmi\_tlv\_tag values match, and the pdev/vdev param numbers match \(PMF\_QOS 34, DYNAMIC\_BW 10, TX\_ENCAP 48, RX\_DECAP 91\). Hypothesis \(unverified\): this firmware evaluates vdev create with the 28-byte TLV, the 8 extra bytes make it fail, and it asserts in whal\_wmac\_recipes.c:whal\_wmac\_transition\_to\_fw\_c instead of returning credits.
- 2026-11-07 device readback of DA4FC79592169588 \(HTC trailer trace\) corrects the credit story: the firmware DOES send ATH11K\_HTC\_RECORD\_CREDITS \(trailer id 1, len 4\) and the port parses it, giving 13 credit returns of 1 each between 58.87 s and 59.125 s. The returns then stop permanently immediately after an MSI on vector 3 \(CE3\). 5435 subsequent sends report 'need 1 have 0', WMI command 20488 times out three times and 20482 once, selftest scan returns -11, and the firmware asserts in whal\_wmac\_recipes.c:whal\_wmac\_transition\_to\_fw\_c. msi mask stays 0000000e with ce0-8 counts 0 1 21 1, so interrupts keep arriving while no command is consumed.
- 2026-11-07 device readback of 951A343605E803D3 quantifies the WMI stall: htc target credits 1 size 2176, Control/HTT get 0, WMI gets 1, and after the first command 5469 consecutive 'htc ep 2 short of credits: need 1 have 0' entries accumulate with no credit report processed anywhere in the boot \(no credit report line and no 'Unhandled record' warning\). Three wmi command timeouts \(20488 VDEV\_SET\_PARAM, 20482 VDEV\_DELETE\) follow and the self-test scan returns -11; the firmware asserts in whal\_wmac\_recipes.c:whal\_wmac\_transition\_to\_fw\_c. The total-credit value of 1 comes from ath11k\_htc\_wait\_target's supports\_shadow\_regs clamp \(WCN6750 has supports\_shadow\_regs = true\), which is upstream behaviour.
- 2026-11-07 device readback of EA1DA3D02D2354B9: the default wlan interface is present \(station primary present, stage 4\) and the machine reaches the desktop, but the board self-test scan fails with -11 before any scan command reaches ath11k\_mac\_op\_hw\_scan. wmi\_cmd\_send's tx\_credit wait \(3 s\) expires three times for WMI\_VDEV\_SET\_PARAM\_CMDID and once for WMI\_VDEV\_DELETE\_CMDID \(20482=0x5002\); the firmware then asserts in whal\_wmac\_recipes.c:whal\_wmac\_transition\_to\_fw\_c. msi mask rises from 0 to 0000000e, so CE1/2/3 deliver interrupts. No scan, association or DHCP evidence exists in this boot.
- 2026-11-07 device readback of A7E3489A864DF276 confirms the initcall diagnosis: control transport installed, cfg80211 init 0, mac80211 init 0 at 51.12 s, and zero 'ieee80211 registration failed' or 'failed register the radio' lines in the whole boot \(previously the terminal failure\). Registration advanced into device\_add and stopped at 'bug at shim/src/lx\_rt.c:484', the get\_device BUG\_ON, because nw\_ahb.c's platform\_device\_register\_full only set of\_node and never initialised g\_pdev.dev, which ath11k hands to mac80211 as the wiphy parent. The system was not wedged \(log reaches 338 s\), but pdev registration did not complete and fw ready stopped at pdev\_create.
- 2026-11-07 root cause of ieee80211\_register\_hw -EINVAL: not the HE-40MHz consistency check. Device readback of 40170704B812E936 shows both sides agreeing \(phy2g0 carries bit 0x02, band-1 phy0 carries 0x04, ht40 1 he40 1, raw WMI TLV len 236\), and HT40 alignment never fired. Instruction-level scanning of kernel-final.macho shows zero callers for lx\_init\_cfg80211\_init, lx\_init\_ieee80211\_init, lx\_root\_init and nw\_wifi\_control\_transport\_set, while the same scanner finds the expected caller for ieee80211\_register\_hw. Their only upstream caller is nw\_wifi\_start in src/nw\_wifi.c, which build-ath11k-wifi.py excludes. Without cfg80211\_init, wiphy\_sysfs\_init never runs, ieee80211\_class is unregistered, and device\_add returns -EINVAL from shim/src/lx\_rt.c:508.
- 2026-11-06 mac80211 registration -EINVAL localized to the one silent return in ieee80211\_register\_hw \(Linux 7.0.14 main.c:1306-1313: an HE station entry declaring HT 40 MHz without the matching HE PHY 40 MHz bit\). Every other -EINVAL in that function sits inside WARN\_ON and would have printed through lx\_warn; the boot log had zero 'warning at' lines, and disassembly shows only two bare mov w0,\#-0x16 \(the shared silent return and ieee80211\_init\_cipher\_suites, which cannot fire because ath11k sets cipher\_suites\). ath11k sets IEEE80211\_HT\_CAP\_SUP\_WIDTH\_20\_40 unconditionally whenever the firmware enables HT, so the two sides can disagree on this firmware.
- 2026-11-06 MSI dispatch verified on device \(1115B4ECEC8AC40E\): decoding the sink inside lx\_platform.poll delivers the firmware interrupt through the pump nested in every synchronous wait, so HTC target and HTT connect complete \(no timeout lines\) and the whole system stays up. The AHB hybrid hif ops still lack get\_ce\_msi\_idx, so CE5's doorbell \(0x325\) is dispatched to CE7's handler; harmless during bring-up but must be fixed before WMI traffic. ath11k\_core\_pdev\_create then fails with -EINVAL from ieee80211\_register\_hw; the reachable silent branch is mac80211's HE-40MHz consistency check, and zero lx\_warn output rules out every WARN\_ON validation.
- 2026-11-06: seven source repositories branched from the latest local GPU checkpoint. WPSS board glue and upstream protocol cores are integrated into the formal kernel; network data-path implementation remains planned.
- B883 logs both CE0\_DST\_CTRL and REO1\_MISC as 0x170acce5. This does not establish usable registers. Follow-up streams modem FAT REGDB and O82 BDF after exact length and SHA256 checks. The original ath11k 0x25 descriptors handle 4 REGDB and 10 BDF segments. Firmware init indications and before/after MMIO values are recorded.
- 82C2 device accepted IND\_REGISTER/HOST\_CAP and returned CAP 0x1d without a board ID, plus BAR 0x61e00000 length 0x200000. The follow-up reads only REO1\_MISC and CE0\_DST\_CTRL for that exact BAR tuple. OEM module analysis indicates bd\_o82.elf for product11/CN with no firmware board ID.
- 49A7 device returned a matched CAP transaction with result1/error90 \(INCOMPATIBLE\_STATE\). The follow-up uses the original ath11k fixed-memory IND\_REGISTER/HOST\_CAP order before CAP/DEVICE\_INFO. The peer fixture checks the original wire fields and success gates.
- 983B device readback confirms signed load and PAS reset, stage11/slave6, IPCRTR link1 and WLFW 45/13/8/301. There are129 new valid slots against the D774 readback.
- The read-only observer issues CAP 0x24 then DEVICE\_INFO 0x4c. The original decoder gets only bounded TLVs and matched endpoint/transaction responses. The peer check covers normal replies, stale replies, timeout, malformed replies and firmware errors.
- All21 upstream ath11k core/AHB translation units compile for arm64e iPadOS27. Kernel integration currently uses the identity codec only. Board platform, SMMU/DMA and MSI imports remain to be implemented for the full backend.
- D774 device photo identifies PC0xfffffff00b6aa228 as bzero+0x48/DC ZVA and LR0xfffffff00b63f5c0 as loadSegments+0x208. Candidate is DO\_NOT\_FLASH. Recovery readback contains1 new valid slot/2047 unchanged and ends at52.600944s, with no Wi-Fi-stage record; it does not establish WPSS progress.
- The repair replaces only mapped WPSS destination copies/zeroing with volatile aligned32-bit stores and byte heads/tails. One focused ASan/UBSan check loads all11 stock segments across the full25MiB guarded reservation and verifies file bytes, zero BSS, untouched gaps and guard-boundary tails. Mapping, metadata and PAS error handling remain unchanged; device acceptance pending.
- The focused ASan/UBSan check verifies78 original file pins and17 unchanged protocol files; the actual firmware parses as ELF32,11 nonoverlapping load segments,8198565 file bytes,26214400-byte span, and4844 metadata bytes identical to stock MDT.
- 2026-11-06 source review: stock live DTS, Uke DTBO and qca\_cld3\_qca6750.ko identify WCN6750 with WPSS and SMEM GLINK IPCRTR. Ath12kWifi main28de7b91 and sheng main46017956 target WCN7850 PCIe/MHI and cannot be enabled as a drop-in hardware driver. Public mac80211/cfg80211, 24A446 WifiNet, supplicant and Qcom transport helpers are reuse candidates; WCN6750 needs an ath11k AHB/hybrid backend. That initial source-review phase performed no runtime integration or hardware validation.
- 2026-11-06 90C23: the stock regulatory database was accepted in full and the board file stalled at its third segment with no QMI reply. Read-only apps-SMMU reports show sCR0=0x002e0406 \(USFCFG set\), gfsr=0, idr0=0x4c017e6e, idr1=0x60000047 \(71 banks, 128 global pages\) and no stream-match entry for WLAN SID0x1400. The missing mapping does not by itself raise a recorded fault, so the BDF stall remains unresolved.
- 2026-11-06 SMMU writes are unsafe on this platform: two bypass route candidates \(8CA85D custom masked SMR, 6CF650 touch-proven helper\) froze the whole system during Wi-Fi start, while the read-only survey C155848 booted to the desktop. Do not retry SMR/S2CR modifications under live storage/touch traffic; a real stage-1 context for SID0x1400 is the documented DMA path.
- 2026-11-06 official-doc alignment: porting/network prescribes nw\_abi/nw\_wifi/WifiProbe/WifiNet and a DMA reachability checkpoint before scan; porting/platform requires PA-to-IOVA, SMMU instance/stream ownership and cache policy per DMA device. uke currently implements only the WPSS/QMI hardware-control layer; the ABI, worker, OS interface and DMA/MSI checkpoints are still missing.

### 完成条件

- WPSS ready and WLFW discovery, then ath11k DMA/MSI and scanning, then 24A446 WifiNet/supplicant association, DHCP and sustained bidirectional traffic, each with device evidence.

### 当前范围

- Wi-Fi lifecycle coverage remains separate for repeated cold boots, toggle/reconnect, sleep/wake, error cases and long-run power behavior.

### 下一步

- Extend Wi-Fi lifecycle coverage and correlate any recurrence with same-boot scan, association, DHCP and traffic logs.

## 音频 {#audio}

`implemented`

### 硬件与依赖

- Uke Cliffs ADSP PAS1 and DTB PAS36, reserved 0x9d400000/0x4500000 and 0xa1900000/0x80000, stock modem split firmware.
- Sheng upstream 6bc0ce1e8c3a3733110a80dd80ddfb2a1ca46555 and Senemos fd33b9174872395fc215bfad5127e525eb423467.

### 配置

- First stage uses adsp=2 battery=1 audio=1. Audio mode1 performs APM readiness only. Existing board defaults keep ADSP disabled until the explicit staged payload is selected.

### 操作步骤

- Run python3 scripts/check-uke-dsp-host.py, formal kernel and staged payload builds. After candidate authorization, capture same-boot UKE ADSP, UKE BATTERY and UKE AUDIO logs.

### 开发经验

- 2026-11-15: The shared DSP host harness now also runs the battery recovery and timer tests. ADSP shutdown and shared SCM WAITQ checks pass.
- Shared IPCC/QMP/RPMh and serialized firmware/SMEM access implemented. ADSP GLINK includes QRTR service locator, PMIC and bounded GPR readiness callbacks. Upstream AudioReach command codec is compiled; no PCM endpoint is published.

### 完成条件

- Direct four-speaker PCM and four-channel capture with correct clocks, routing, DMA progress and teardown, then the 24A446 HAL and OS applications. DSP restart and resource recovery must be qualified separately.

### 当前范围

- 259E and3EAE logs confirm ADSP/GLINK/APM readiness; D4EF battery percentage and charging UI are user-verified. D4EF same-boot counters, accuracy, cable transitions and long/recovery checks remain pending. FS19xx/WCD939x stream engines, HAL and automatic DSP restart remain unfinished. Stock preset, ACDB, firmware version and split-file hashes were captured read-only on 2026-11-11. Linux host lacks complete CoreAudio HAL SDK headers.

### 下一步

- D4EF battery percentage/charging UI user-verified. Preserve accepted release. Proceed with FS19xx four-speaker direct PCM, WCD939x four-channel capture, DMA/clock/preset checks and cleanup before HAL. Battery same-boot counters, precision, transitions and long/recovery checks stay separate; each new flash needs current authorization.

## 电源 {#power}

`device-verified`

组件: `Adreno740`, `AppleKeyStoreState`.

### 硬件与依赖

- PMGR power policy and the GMU voltage/clock voting over the AOSS/RPMH path.
- Hardware watchdog policy \(sheng-disable-watchdogd=1\) and the gh\_virt\_wdt pet.

### 配置

- Kernel arguments sheng-pmgr=2 sheng-lite=1 sheng-aks=1 sheng-disable-watchdogd=1.
- GMU core 220 MHz / hub 200 MHz with the validated vote sequence.

### 操作步骤

- Boot the staged power modes and read the GX/S0 heartbeat rows.

### 开发经验

- 2026-11-15: Battery request timeout recovery and its host checks are committed. A failed field invalidates only that field, refresh and subscription recover, and a queued reply is consumed before the timer checks the active deadline.
- 2026-11-14 电池长期刷新停更确认：同boot77C55B96，184–2408s ans976/to1固定，rx和ignored同增590，工作循环继续。一次超时后的poisoned永久隔离和刷新取消缺少自动恢复；首条超时已覆盖，字段及底层触发原因未知。见docs/BATTERY-REFRESH-20261114.md。
- 2026-11-11 D4EF battery percentage and charging display basic UI confirmed by user; no new device logs captured in the acceptance turn. Firmware charging control unchanged.
- Mode 8 passed: GX off/on and the perf vote acked with the heartbeat H0.. every 2 s.
- The scm secure-watchdog path is a dead end; the gh\_virt\_wdt pet is accepted and disable is refused.
- 2026-11-10 input/backlight: reused sheng maina68ee4ee and core BacklightMode54796a7, adapted uke PON/GPIO6 and dual KTZ8866. ASan/UBSan5082 host checks and owner runtime signature/original-section/negative-page checks passed. Formal kernel/payload/13-area/FAT/signature/UEFI-roundtrip checks passed; native2x candidate A79E83AEA028FE78/vendor\_boot9ad565a1a01a529c61f4b7baffd256ca301725963d3ce54fe58572456ac7d17b is unflashed. Display225 companion inherits prior display failures and is DO\_NOT\_FLASH; no hardware verification or device operations. Existing device-verified status belongs to earlier accepted power functionality, not new buttons/backlight.

### 完成条件

- GPU power states transition and the board keeps running with the watchdog policy applied.

### 当前范围

- Suspend/resume is not implemented \(the ADT defaults pin sleep-disabled=1\).
- Power/volume physical keys, CoreBrightness slider and screen-off/on backlight restoration are hardware-unverified; SoC deep suspend remains out of scope.

### 下一步

- Verify battery percentage, charging transitions, and continued refresh under plug/unplug and long-run tests on the deployed recovery build.

## 镜像组装 {#packaging}

`host-verified`

组件: `DarwinBoot`, `XnuPrelink`, `VerifiedRoot`.

### 硬件与依赖

- Stock vendor\_boot DTB with the board's dtb\_node patches \(splash reservation, payload at 0x1000000\).
- Trust cache bundle and the recorded Data seed.

### 配置

- 160MiB raw FAT, payload.compression=uefi; EDK II standard UEFI compression transport in vendor\_boot. Paired uke UEFI expands into reserved0xA8000000..0xB2000000 after overlap checks.
- Android v4 vendor\_boot retains original DTB/table/bootconfig/vbmeta. Accepted boot1519616B and vendor\_boot44859392B fit the96MiB partitions.

### 操作步骤

- ./unixnu build payload --kernel kernel.macho --unique-chip-id HEX -D ../UniXNU-uke-build
- ./unixnu bootimg --uefi ../uefi-uke --dtb ../uke-port/data/vendor\_boot.dtb --ramdisk none -D ../UniXNU-uke-build

### 开发经验

- 2026-11-13 AppSync 0d3444d4 boot failed: CF508159 same-boot logs show three backboardd dyld missing NSConstantDoubleNumber exits, then recovery teardown with photographed vnode usecount underflow. Restored stock four boot partitions, slot A, TWRP; read-only collection, no host userdata writes. Reexport fix candidate vendor\_boot SHA256 047a13e34d8f50cc37c6e34897baf5af782309e5cb040a7d7f97b8bfaad52825 is host-verified; user-authorized flash completed for all four boot partitions, slot A confirmed, reboot accepted. User subsequently reported reaching the desktop with no other issue in this observation. AppSync is a background adapter without a home-screen app; actual user-app installation, launch and reboot retention remain unverified. Evidence: uke-port/releases/2026-11-13-appsync-reexport/user-observation.json. Receipt: uke-port/releases/2026-11-13-appsync-reexport/flash-result-20261113-214541.json. Actual Foundation LC\_REEXPORT\_DYLIB is checked; only AppSync dylib and its trust change, full packed kernel preserved byte-for-byte. Independent APFS unload reference ownership remains unresolved. See docs/APPSYNC-20261113.md.
- 2026-11-13: AppSync integration ports sheng c3c7468 and 20cf25d, reusing the existing core adapter and in-process signer. Linux builder preserves the prior addon/trust modules and Uke direct brightness transactions. Formal kernel/prelink/payload and paired image verification passed; vendor\_boot SHA256 0d3444d47650507b510dae5e8befaa19e678b6cf9b0a5579656218232c46f4f2. 55 AppSync files added, all prior addon files and trust modules preserved; 842B args, eight cores. 2026-11-13 user-authorized flash completed for boot\_a/vendor\_boot\_a/boot\_b/vendor\_boot\_b; slot A confirmed and reboot accepted. Boot UI, installation/launch/update/reboot and backlight/Wi-Fi still require Uke hardware verification. Receipt: uke-port/releases/2026-11-13-appsync/flash-result-20261113-213230.json. See docs/APPSYNC-20261113.md.
- The v4 header parsing and the existing-/chosen tolerance are in unixnu bootimg \(core 5525d18\); the boot image carries an empty ramdisk because the payload is the vendor\_boot vendor ramdisk.
- payload, boot, vendor\_boot and userdata all build on Linux; only the Data skip-setup seed still needs macOS.

### 完成条件

- payload.img contains kernel.macho, its ADT, the trust cache, the Data seed and kernel.macho.vroot; boot.img boots from the stock DTB.

### 当前范围

- Exact binary identity is the accepted release manifest and CRC90B6BC701210D9DB; later documentation/pin commits do not imply a rebuilt image.

### 下一步

- After candidateauthorization deploy paired19948EA4D7EDD158,verifyvsynchz120/start1/refresh1,DPUflip/latch anddesktop tearing,then&gt;8minStageManager andperipheral regression.6D31DO\_NOT\_FLASH.

## 诊断 {#diagnostics}

`host-verified`

组件: `AppleKeyStoreState`, `XnuPrelink`.

### 硬件与依赖

- Embedded panic log at 0xFF800000/0x380000 read by the menu before XNU clears it.
- Boot progress stages and the uke log ring \(sheng-log=1\).

### 配置

- panic\_screen true with the vendored Font.h; boot-progress=2 for the stage bars.
- UKE MEM counters \(gUkeMemStatusGuid\) and the previous panic line on the menu.

### 操作步骤

- Boot with boot-progress=2 and read the menu status, then the kernel log.

### 开发经验

- 2026-11-15: Periodic CPU, battery and touch statistics are gated by the requested diagnostic settings; basic persistent error logging remains available.
- The panic screen, ramoops print and the last-panic line work; the jetsam OOM vs corpse messages are distinguished in the log.
- The migration's own diagnostics: from-scratch kernel builds and the ADT baseline diff run on the host.

### 完成条件

- Failures leave a readable panic line on screen and in the log.

### 当前范围

- Prior failing builds have same-boot UKELLOG1 and Data captures. Current normal-use results come from the device user. The archived older TRACE readback covers all eight CPU counters through 2408 seconds.

### 下一步

- On recurrence collect the same-boot text/panic ring and CPU timer/IPI/VM/Data state. Keep timing diagnostics in TRACE mode and preserve panic text before display output.
